D-0011 — `superclaude eval doctor` subcommand spec\n\n**Task:** T01.13 (Phase 1, Roadmap FR-CLI4 / R-011)\n**Module:** `src/superclaude/cli/eval/commands.py`\n**CLI surface:** `superclaude eval doctor [--json] [--no-mcp] [--check-coverage]`\n**Status:** Implemented 2026-05-20\n\n## Command surface\n\n| Flag | Type | Effect |\n|---|---|---|\n| `--json` | bool flag | Emit a JSON payload extending the `CapabilityReport` contract; stdout only. |\n| `--no-mcp` | bool flag | Forwarded to `CapabilityGates(skip_flags={\"--no-mcp\"})` so SOFT-SKIP MCP rows are flag-overridden. |\n| `--check-coverage` | bool flag | Reserved for FR-G5 coverage gate (M4 T04.14); for M1 it stamps a `coverage_gate` marker into the JSON payload and prints a deferral note in the human checklist. |\n\nExit codes:\n\n* `0` — + description + detail.\n\n## Green-checklist format\n\nRendered by `render_checklist(report)`:\n\n```\nsuperclaude eval doctor:\n  [ok] <description> -- <detail>\n  [--] <description> (SOFT-SKIP) -- <detail>\n  [XX] <description> (HARD) -- <detail>\nskip flags: <…>            # only when CapabilityReport.skip_flags is non-empty\nall HARD capabilities satisfied | HARD failures: <names>\nsoft skips: <names>        # only when there are no HARD failures\n```\n\nRow markers are emitted by `_row_marker(row)` and never use shell colour\ncodes so the output is safe to capture in CI logs.\n\n## JSON payload schema\n\nThe payload is `CapabilityReport.to_json()` plus a single extension key:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"report\":         [ {CapabilityStatus rows…} ],\n  \"blocked_evals\":  [],\n  \"skip_flags\":     [],\n  \"hard_failures\":  [],\n  \"soft_skips\":     [],\n  \"soft_xfails\":    [],\n  \"coverage_gate\": {\n    \"requested\":  bool,\n    \"status\":     \"deferred\",\n    \"milestone\":  \"M4\",\n    \"task\":       \"T04.14\"\n  }\n}\n```\n\n`json.dumps(payload, indent=2, sort_keys=True)` guarantees byte-level\ndeterminism across invocations on a stable host (verified by\n`test_cli_doctor_json_is_deterministic_across_invocations`).\n\nThe `report[]` list ordering is fixed: the seven default capability rows\n(from `_DEFAULT_CAPABILITY_SPECS`) in declaration order, followed by the\nthree doctor-specific rows in this fixed order:\n\n1. `claude.min_version`     — HARD\n2. `filesystem.claude_home` — HARD\n3. `vendored.ptytest`       — SOFT-SKIP\n\n## Supplementary fails closed when binary missing, output unparseable, or below floor. |\n| `filesystem.claude_home` | hard | `Path.home() / \".claude\"` is_dir | Override via `claude_home=` for tests. |\n| `vendored.ptytest` | skip | `<cli/eval>/pty/__init__.py` is_file | SOFT-SKIP until M2 vendoring task; lets M1 doctor exit 0 on a clean dev machine. |\n\n## HARD-failure artifact\n\nWhen `report.hard_failures` is non-empty, `render_hard_failure_artifact`\nemits the following stderr block (the \"artifact\" mandated by FR-CLI4\nAC):\n\n```\neval doctor: HARD failures\n  - <name> (<description>): <detail>\n  - <name> (<description>): <detail>\n```\n\nThe artifact is emitted regardless of `--json` / human mode so CI logs\nalways capture a stable per-failure diagnostic line.\n\n## Test injection seams\n\n| Seam | How tests use it |\n|---|---|\n| `commands._default_claude_version_probe` | `monkeypatch.setattr` to return a literal version banner or `None`. |\n| `shutil.which` | `monkeypatch.setattr` to fake PATH state without touching the host. |\n| `Path.home` | `monkeypatch.setattr` to redirect `~/.claude/` resolution to a tmp dir. |\n| `build_doctor_report(gates=…, claude_version_probe=…, claude_home=…, pty_dir=…)` | Direct kwargs for unit tests that need full control of every probe. |\n\n## Wiring\n\n* `eval_group` added to `superclaude.cli.main:main` via\n  `main.add_command(eval_group, name=\"eval\")`.\n* `commands.eval_group` and `commands.doctor` use Click 8.3-compatible\n  decorators (no removed `mix_stderr` kwarg).\n\n## Acceptance criteria → implementation map\n\n| AC bullet (T01.13) | Implementation site |\n|---|---|\n| `superclaude eval doctor` exits 0 on a clean dev machine. | `doctor` Click handler; _evalspec_to_dict(spec: EvalSpec) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Project an ``EvalSpec`` into a manifest-shaped dict.\n\n    Field ordering matches ``suite.schema.json`` so YAML/JSON output is\n    deterministic and round-trips visually against the source manifest.\n    Optional fields are omitted when they hold their dataclass default\n    (e.g. ``category=\"\"``, empty tuples, ``timeout_sec=None``) so the\n    \"no editorial transformation\" guarantee from T01.22 Notes holds: an\n    eval that did not declare ``category:`` in YAML does not gain one\n    here.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    payload: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"id\": spec.id,\n        \"title\": spec.title,\n    }\n    if spec.category:\n        payload[\"category\"] = spec.category\n    if spec.requires:\n        payload[\"requires\"] = list(spec.requires)\n    if spec.timeout_sec is not None:\n        payload[\"timeout_sec\"] = spec.timeout_sec\n    if spec.isolation is not None:\n        payload[\"isolation\"] = dict(spec.isolation)\n    if spec.inputs:\n        payload[\"inputs\"] = [dict(row) for row in spec.inputs]\n    if spec.expects:\n        payload[\"expects\"] = [dict(row) for row in spec.expects]\n    if spec.parameterize:\n        payload[\"parameterize\"] = [dict(row) for row in spec.parameterize]\n    return payload\n\n\ndef _parsed_suite_to_dict(parsed: ParsedSuite) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Project a :class:`ParsedSuite` into the manifest envelope shape.\n\n    ``evals`` is the post-parameterize-expansion list (the canonical\n    \"what would run if you invoked ``eval run``\" view match wins):\n\n    1. ``token`` is itself a file path that exists → return it.\n    2. ``suites_dir / f\"{token}.yaml\"`` exists → return it (filename stem\n       convention used by the built-in suites).\n    3. Otherwise scan ``suites_dir`` and return the first manifest whose\n       schema-validated ``name`` field equals ``token``. Broken manifests\n       in the same directory are skipped silently — we only fail on the\n       *requested* suite, not on the neighbours.\n\n    Raises:\n        SuiteNotFound: No manifest matched the token under any rule. CLI\n            callers map this to :data:`SUITE_NOT_FOUND_EXIT_CODE` (= 2).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    candidate = Path(suite)\n    if candidate.is_file():\n        return candidate\n\n    by_filename = suites_dir / f\"{suite}.yaml\"\n    if by_filename.is_file():\n        return by_filename\n\n    loader = SuiteLoader()\n    for manifest_path in discover_suite_manifests(suites_dir):\n        try:\n            parsed = loader.load(manifest_path)\n        except SuiteLoaderError:\n            # Neighbour manifest is broken; don't let it mask the lookup.\n            continue\n        if parsed.name == suite:\n            return manifest_path\n\n    raise SuiteNotFound(suite, suites_dir)\n\n\ndef describe_suite(\n    suite: str,\n    *,\n    suites_dir: Path,\n    eval_id: Optional[str] = None,\n    loader: Optional[SuiteLoader] = None,\n) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Resolve, load, and project a suite (optionally a single eval).\n\n    Validation runs via :class:`SuiteLoader` BEFORE any rendering, so\n    schema / id-regex / capability rejections raise before the caller\n    can produce stdout (FR-CLI3 AC: validate before print).\n\n    Args:\n        suite: Suite name, filename stem, or direct manifest path.        return _parsed_suite_to_dict(parsed)\n\n    for spec in parsed.evals:\n        if spec.id == eval_id:\n            return _evalspec_to_dict(spec)\n    raise EvalNotFound(\n _evalspec_to_dict(spec: EvalSpec) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Project an ``EvalSpec`` into a manifest-shaped dict.\n\n    Field ordering matches ``suite.schema.json`` so YAML/JSON output is\n    deterministic and round-trips visually against the source manifest.\n    Optional fields are omitted when they hold their dataclass default\n    (e.g. ``category=\"\"``, empty tuples, ``timeout_sec=None``) so the\n    \"no editorial transformation\" guarantee from T01.22 Notes holds: an\n    eval that did not declare ``category:`` in YAML does not gain one\n    here.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    payload: dict[str, Any] = dict(spec.isolation)\n    if spec.inputs:\n        payload[\"inputs\"] = [dict(row) for row in spec.inputs]\n    if spec.expects:\n        payload[\"expects\"] = [dict(row) for row in spec.expects]\n \"\"\"Project a :class:`ParsedSuite` into the manifest envelope shape.\n\n    ``evals`` is the post-parameterize-expansion list (the canonical\n    \"what would run if you invoked ``eval run``\" view _default_claude_version_probe() -> Optional[str]:\n    \"\"\"Invoke ``claude --version`` and return its stdout+stderr or ``None``.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [\"claude\", \"--version\"],\n            check=False,\n            capture_output=True,\n            text=True,\n            timeout=5,\n        )\n    except (FileNotFoundError, OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):\n        return None\n    raw = (result.stdout or \"\") + (result.stderr or \"\")\n    return raw.strip() or None\n\n\ndef _check_claude_version(\n    probe: not None else EvalConfig()\n        min_version = active_config.min_claude_version\n    description = f\"Claude CLI >= {_format_version(min_version)}\"\n    try:\n        raw = probe()\n    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - probe is a user-supplied callable\n        return CapabilityStatus(\n            name=\"claude.min_version\",\n            passed=False,\n           name=\"claude.min_version\",\n            passed=False,\n            failure_mode=\"hard\",\n            description=description,\n            detail=\"claude --version not callable\",\n        )\n    parsed = _parse_version(raw)\n    if parsed is None:\n        return CapabilityStatus(\n            name=\"claude.min_version\",\n            passed=False,\n passed=passed,\n        failure_mode=\"hard\",\n        description=description,\n        detail=detail,\n    )\n\n\ndef _check_claude_home(home: Optional[Path] = None) -> CapabilityStatus:\n tuple[CapabilityStatus, ...],\n) -> CapabilityReport:\n    \"\"\"Return a new ``CapabilityReport`` with ``extras`` appended and\n    failure-bucket tallies updated.\n\n    The base report's     if status.passed:\n            continue\n        if status.failure_mode == \"hard\":\n            hard_failures.append(status.name)\n        elif status.failure_mode == \"skip\":\n _evalspec_to_dict(spec: EvalSpec) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Project an ``EvalSpec`` into a manifest-shaped dict.\n\n    Field ordering matches ``suite.schema.json`` so YAML/JSON output is\n    deterministic and round-trips visually against the source manifest.\n    Optional fields are omitted when they hold their dataclass default\n    (e.g. ``category=\"\"``, empty tuples, ``timeout_sec=None``) so the\n    \"no editorial transformation\" guarantee from T01.22 Notes holds: an\n    eval that did not declare ``category:`` in YAML does not gain one\n    here.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    payload: dict[str, Any] = list(spec.requires)\n    if spec.timeout_sec is not None:\n        payload[\"timeout_sec\"] = spec.timeout_sec\n    if spec.isolation is not None:\n        payload[\"isolation\"] = dict(spec.isolation)\n    if spec.inputs:\n        payload[\"inputs\"] = [dict(row) for row in spec.inputs]\n    if spec.expects:\n        payload[\"expects\"] = [dict(row) for row in spec.expects]\n    if spec.parameterize:\n        payload[\"parameterize\"] = [dict(row) for row in spec.parameterize]\n    return payload\n\n\ndef _parsed_suite_to_dict(parsed: ParsedSuite) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Project a :class:`ParsedSuite` into the manifest envelope shape.\n\n    ``evals`` is the post-parameterize-expansion list (the canonical\n    \"what would run if you invoked ``eval run``\" view per FR-CLI3 AC).\n    Suite-level fields are verbatim from the manifest so the output\n    mirrors the validated source byte-for-byte modulo the ``evals``\n    expansion.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    return {\n        \"name\": parsed.name,\n        \"version\": parsed.version,\n        \"description\": parsed.description,\n        \"defaults\": dict(parsed.defaults),\n        \"required_binaries\": parsed.evals],\n    }\n\n\ndef resolve_suite_manifest(suite: str, suites_dir: Path) -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Resolve ``--suite <token>`` to a manifest path on disk.\n\n    Resolution precedence (first match wins):\n\n    1. ``token`` is itself a file path that exists → return it.\n    2. ``suites_dir / f\"{token}.yaml\"`` exists → return it (filename stem\n       convention used by the built-in suites).\n    3. Otherwise scan ``suites_dir`` and return the first manifest whose\n       schema-validated ``name`` field equals ``token``. Broken manifests\n       in the same directory are skipped silently — we only fail on the\n       *requested* suite, not on the neighbours.\n\n    Raises:\n        SuiteNotFound: No manifest matched the token under any rule. CLI\n            callers map this to :data:`SUITE_NOT_FOUND_EXIT_CODE` (= 2).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    candidate = Path(suite)\n    if candidate.is_file():\n        return candidate\n\n    by_filename = suites_dir / f\"{suite}.yaml\"\n    if by_filename.is_file():\n        return by_filename\n\n    loader = SuiteLoader()\n    for manifest_path in discover_suite_manifests(suites_dir):\n        try:\n            parsed = loader.load(manifest_path)\n        except SuiteLoaderError:\n            # Neighbour manifest is broken; don't let it mask the lookup.\n            continue\n        if parsed.name == suite:\n            return manifest_path\n\n    raise SuiteNotFound(suite, suites_dir)\n\n\ndef describe_suite(\n    suite: str,\n    *,\n    suites_dir: Path,\n    eval_id: Optional[str] = None,\n    loader: Optional[SuiteLoader] = None,\n) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Resolve, load, and project a suite (optionally a single eval).\n\n    Validation runs via :class:`SuiteLoader` BEFORE any rendering, so\n    schema / id-regex / capability rejections raise before the caller\n    can produce stdout (FR-CLI3 AC: validate before print).\n\n    Args:\n        suite: Suite name, filename stem, or direct manifest path. Missing → ``EvalNotFound``.\n        loader: Injectable :class:`SuiteLoader` for tests that need to\n            stub capability resolution.\n\n    Returns:\n        A dict suitable for ``yaml.safe_dump`` / ``json.dumps``. :func:`_evalspec_to_dict` produces).\n\n    Raises:\n        SuiteNotFound: Resolution rule chain produced no manifest.\n        EvalNotFound: ``eval_id`` did not match any post-expansion id.\n        return _parsed_suite_to_dict(parsed)\n\n    for spec in parsed.evals:\n        if spec.id == eval_id:\n            return _evalspec_to_dict(spec)\n    raise EvalNotFound(\n The version floor is recorded\nas:\n\n| Field | Value | Source |\n|---|---|---|\n| `min_version` | `0.5.0` | `superclaude.cli.eval.config.DEFAULT_MIN_CLAUDE_VERSION` |\n| `max_version` | open-ended (no upper bound enforced at M2) | `EvalConfig` field for future override |\n\n`max_version` is intentionally unbounded: the harness pre-commits to\nforward compatibility with future `claude` releases; the field shape\n(`tuple[int, int, int]`) leaves room for a future `max_claude_version`\naddition without breaking callers (deferred per R1 follow-up).\n\nOperators may override the floor by constructing\n`EvalConfig(min_claude_version=(major, minor, patch))`. The doctor\nmodule **does not embed a hard-coded constant** — the\n`test_doctor_module_does_not_define_hardcoded_floor_constant` guard\nasserts the constant `_MIN_CLAUDE_VERSION` is absent.\n\n## Single source of truth\n\n```\n                  +-------------------------------------+\n                  | superclaude.cli.eval.config         |\n                  |   DEFAULT_MIN_CLAUDE_VERSION = (0,5,0)\n                  |   EvalConfig.min_claude_version     |\n                  +-----------------+-------------------+\n                                    |\n                                    v\n       +----------------------------+----------------------------+\n       |          _check_claude_version(config=...)              |\n       |  - probe()  ->  \"claude X.Y.Z\"                          |\n       |  - parse via _VERSION_RE                                |\n       |  - compare parsed >= config.min_claude_version          |\n       |  - CapabilityStatus(name=\"claude.min_version\", hard)    |\n       +----------------------------+----------------------------+\n                                    |\n                                    v\n                      +-------------+-------------+\n                      | build_doctor_report(...)  |\n                      |   forwards config kwarg   |\n                      +-------------+-------------+\n                                    |\n                                    v\n                       eval doctor / --json payload\n                       (exit 2 on below-floor stub)\n```\n\n## Surface changes\n\n### `src/superclaude/cli/eval/config.py`\n\n* New module-level constant `DEFAULT_MIN_CLAUDE_VERSION = (0, 5, 0)`.\n* New `EvalConfig` field\n  `min_claude_version: tuple[int, int, int] = DEFAULT_MIN_CLAUDE_VERSION`.\n* `__all__` extended with `\"DEFAULT_MIN_CLAUDE_VERSION\"`.\n\n### `src/superclaude/cli/eval/commands.py`\n\n* `_MIN_CLAUDE_VERSION` removed (the doctor module no longer embeds a\n  duplicate of the policy).\n* `_check_claude_version(probe=None, min_version=None, *, config=None)`\n  now sources its floor from `config.min_claude_version` when\n  `min_version` is not supplied; falls back to a default-constructed\n  `EvalConfig`. The legacy explicit-kwarg seam is preserved for tests\n  that need to override the floor without constructing a config.\n* `build_doctor_report(..., config: EvalConfig | None = None)`\n  forwards the config to `_check_claude_version`. By moving\n   it into `EvalConfig`, tests construct a strict / permissive config\n   and exercise the boundary without monkeypatching module state.\n\n## Why keep `min_version=` as an explicit kwarg\n\nThe legacy `_check_claude_version(probe=…, min_version=…)` signature is\nstill in use across the existing `tests/cli/eval/test_doctor.py`\nfixtures.
